### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given integral is <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
Let, <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sec</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mi></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
Applying <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> property and adding, we get,
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>min</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mn>6</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/cc62e607-787c-4709-8cdf-a60602da5ab8.png" style="width:2.90694in;height:1.39375in" />
<math><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo></math> shaded area
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>⋅</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⋅</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⋅</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="true">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>, then <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

In <math><mi>A</mi></math>, let <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
In <math><mi>B</mi></math>, let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>u</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>u</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>1</mn></msubsup><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>u</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>u</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>A</mi><mi>n</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mrow><mi>n</mi><mi>π</mi></mrow></munderover><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>N</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∑</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>100</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>110</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>55</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>105</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>110</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>As the period of <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#960;</mi></math>, hence</p><p><math><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>110</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the definite integral <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>7</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>7</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math>, then 
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>7</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>7</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is an odd function
Hence, <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>R</mi></math> is a function defined as  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>,</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> where [.] denotes the greatest integer function, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></msubsup><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/3858171e-8653-41ae-a12b-e238cd332dd0-image25.jpeg" style="height: 154px; width: 330px;" />
We know that,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>[</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="}"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>[</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="}"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Hence, <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> = <math><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></msubsup><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>6</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Applying <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and adding, we get,
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
Again, applying <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and adding, we get,</p><p> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msubsup><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>6</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>6</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>6</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>6</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></mrow></mrow></math> then <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the integral <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the integral <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi>A</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Applying <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> property in <math><mi>β</mi><mo>,</mo></math> we get <math><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
Adding both, we get <math><mn>2</mn><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> <math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>I</mi><mi>n</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mrow><mi>n</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munderover><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>, then <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> has the value <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><msup><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>;</mo></math> where <math><mi>&#955;</mi></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/wertyu3456789oiug.JPG" style="height: 153px; width: 220px;" />
max <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⋅</mo><mi>π</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo></math> <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>n</mi><msup><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> (area of triangle)
<math><mo>∴</mo></math> <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>8</mn></munderover><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>32</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **8**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>32</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>8</mn></munderover><mfrac><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>32</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></munderover><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></munderover><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Adding both equations, we get,</p><p> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>8</mn></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>32</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>32</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>α</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>∫</mo><mi>α</mi><msup><mi>α</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></munderover><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mi>x</mi></mfrac></math> (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>α</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>), then the value of <math><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∑</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo></mrow></mrow><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∑</mo><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>&#945;</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#945;</mi><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
Thus, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munderover><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></munderover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>r</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and</p><p> <math><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math><math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, their sum equals to zero</p>

</details>

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> (where [.] denotes greatest integer function) is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0
- **(B)** 1
- **(C)** 2
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) 1**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><munderover><mstyle displaystyle="true" mathsize="140%"><mo>∫</mo></mstyle><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></munderover><mrow><mo>(</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>−</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mstyle displaystyle="true" mathsize="140%"><mo>∫</mo></mstyle><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></munderover><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>−</mo><munderover><mstyle displaystyle="true" mathsize="140%"><mo>∫</mo></mstyle><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></munderover><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></math>

<math><mrow><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></msubsup><mo>−</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><mrow><munderover><mstyle displaystyle="true" mathsize="140%"><mo>∫</mo></mstyle><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>0</mn></munderover><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><munderover><mstyle displaystyle="true" mathsize="140%"><mo>∫</mo></mstyle><mn>0</mn><mn>1</mn></munderover><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>−</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><mrow><munderover><mstyle displaystyle="true" mathsize="140%"><mo>∫</mo></mstyle><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>0</mn></munderover><mrow><mo>(</mo><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><munderover><mstyle displaystyle="true" mathsize="140%"><mo>∫</mo></mstyle><mn>0</mn><mn>1</mn></munderover><mn>0.</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mrow><mtext>If</mtext></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mtext>If</mtext></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo><msubsup><mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>0</mn></msubsup><mo>−</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>π</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math></p><p>As, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mi>a</mi></msubsup><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>a</mi></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>1</mn></msubsup><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>x</mi></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>1</mn></msubsup><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>0</mn><mn>1</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></math> are in

**Options:**

- **(A)** Arithmetic Progression
- **(B)** Geometric Progression
- **(C)** Harmonic Progression
- **(D)** Arithmetic Geometric Progression

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Arithmetic Progression**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></munderover><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></munderover><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></munderover><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><menclose notation="updiagonalstrike"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></menclose></mrow><mrow><menclose notation="updiagonalstrike"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></menclose></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="" separators="|"><mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><menclose notation="updiagonalstrike"><mn>2</mn></menclose><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><menclose notation="updiagonalstrike"><mn>2</mn></menclose><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> They are in <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math> <mrow> <mstyle displaystyle="true"> <mrow> <msubsup> <mo>∫</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mi>π</mi> </msubsup> <mrow> <mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mstyle displaystyle="true"> <msubsup> <mo>∑</mo> <mrow> <mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> <mn>3</mn> </msubsup> <mrow> <msub> <mi>a</mi> <mi>r</mi> </msub> <msup> <mrow> <mi>cos</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mo>−</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mstyle><mi>x</mi><msup> <mrow> <mi>sin</mi></mrow> <mi>r</mi> </msup> <mi>x</mi></mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mrow> </mstyle></mrow> </math> depends upon

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1 </mn></mrow></msub></math>and <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0 </mn></mrow></msub></math>and<math><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2 </mn></mrow></msub></math>and<math><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1 </mn></mrow></msub></math>and<math><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1 </mn></mrow></msub></math>and<math><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∑</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi> </mi></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi> </mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>+</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi> </mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi> </mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>

Since, <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi> </mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> Integral Ist and IIIrd become zero.

<math><mo>∴</mo></math>The given integral depends upon<math><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>and<math><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>.

</details>

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math> <mrow> <munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mrow> <mn>10</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mfrac> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow> </mrow> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>−</mo><mn>28</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>196</mn> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow> </mrow> </mfrac> <mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo> </mrow> </math> where <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the greatest integer less than or equal to <math><mi>x</mi></math>, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>28</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>196</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> ........(i)

Use <math><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>4</mn><mn>10</mn></munderover><mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>14</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>14</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> ......(ii)

(i) + (ii)

we get <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>4</mn><mn>10</mn></munderover><mfrac><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>14</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>14</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> <math><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>π</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></msubsup><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>x</mi></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>x</mi></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a quadratic polynomial with vertex <math><mi>V</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the integral <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>As vertex is <math><mi>V</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, thus the quadratic is symmetric about <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Replacing <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Applying <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> in <math><mi>I</mi></math> and adding, we get,
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>16</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>16</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
(As <math><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is an odd function)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mn>4</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the integral <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> has the value <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **9.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

Applying <math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></math> and adding,
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>19</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>19</mn></math>
Hence, <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9.5</mn></math>

</details>
