### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the value of the definite integral <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the absolute value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to (where, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8901;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> is the greatest integer function)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

As period of <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is <math><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo></math>
<math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mn>0</mn><mi></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which of the following statements is correct?

**Options:**

- **(A)** If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is an odd function, then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is an even function.
- **(B)** If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is an odd function, then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is also odd.
- **(C)** If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a periodic function, then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is also periodic.
- **(D)** If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is periodic, then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is always non <math><mo>-</mo></math> periodic.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is an odd function, then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is an even function.**

**Detailed Solution:**

As, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
Put <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></math>
<math><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></math>
(as <math> <mrow> <mtext>f</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>x</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is odd)
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is even.
Also, Consider <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Which is non <math><mo>-</mo></math> periodic even though <math><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is periodic
Also, if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
Then, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo></math> which is periodic.

</details>

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The integral <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="true">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>, (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mo>.</mo></mfenced></math> represents the greatest integer function) has the value <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>K</mi><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>K</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>p</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>101</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>99</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>100</mn><mi>π</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>99</mn><mi>π</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>99</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The given integral is <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi><mi></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi></mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></munderover><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi></mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></munderover><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>99</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>100</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>π</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>50</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>π</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>25</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>π</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>π</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>50</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>π</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

As the period of <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></math>
So, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>50</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
Applying <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> property and adding, we get,
<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
(As <math><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> )
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>&#960;</mi></math>

</details>

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0**

**Detailed Solution:**

Applying <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> property,
<math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to (where <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the fractional part function)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q48. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munderover><mo>∫</mo><mi>π</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mo>[</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to (where [.] represents the greatest integer function)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mo>&minus;</mo><mi>&pi;</mi> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn><mi>&pi;</mi> </mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mo>&minus;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&pi;</mi> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math> <mrow> <mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mstyle displaystyle="true"> <mrow> <msubsup> <mo>∫</mo> <mi>π</mi> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> </msubsup> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle> </mrow> </math>
 </p><p>For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo> </mo><mi>or</mi><mo> </mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>≤</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></math></p><p><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>

For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mstyle displaystyle="true"> <mrow><munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mi>π</mi> <mrow> <mn>7</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>6</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"> <mrow><munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mrow> <mn>7</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>6</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>11</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>6</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"> <mrow><munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mrow> <mn>11</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>6</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> </munderover> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext> </mtext><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>7</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>6</mn> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>11</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>6</mn> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>7</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>6</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>11</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>6</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mi>π</mi> <mn>6</mn> </mfrac> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>8</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>6</mn> </mfrac> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mi>π</mi> <mn>6</mn> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>10</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>6</mn> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math></p>

</details>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><munderover><mo>∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><mi>π</mi></munderover><msqrt><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo></math> is greatest integer function) is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></munderover><msqrt><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></munderover><msqrt><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>8</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>8</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>[</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> </p>

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of the integral <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>8</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

As <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="false"><mo>→</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mover><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>  
<math><mo>∴</mo></math> Using <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> <math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>1</mn></msubsup><msup><mi>e</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msup><mo>&#8901;</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>1</mn></msubsup><msup><mi>e</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
Put <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math> (in LHS)
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
So, <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></munderover><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>6</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

In <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2,</mn></mrow></msub></math>
let, <math><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math> or <math><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow></math>
Also, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> &amp; <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> are both positive as
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>∀</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>4</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let the value of integral is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi></math>, then,
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> (applying <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math>)
Adding, we get,</p><p><math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The integral <math> <mrow> <mstyle> <mrow><munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mrow> <mo></mo><mo></mo><mo></mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mrow> <mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>ℓ</mi><mi>n</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn></mn><mn></mn><mn></mn><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle> </mrow> </math> is equal to  <math><mo>(</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is the greatest integer <math><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mi>ℓ</mi><mi>n</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math> <mrow> <mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mstyle> <mrow><munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mrow> <mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>ℓ</mi><mi>n</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mstyle> <mrow><munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mstyle> <mrow><munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mrow> <mi>ℓ</mi><mi>n</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mstyle> <mrow><munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> <mn>0</mn> </munderover> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mstyle> <mrow><munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle> </mrow> </mrow> </mstyle> </mrow> </math> <span>                       <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mo>∵</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mi>log</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>is</mi><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>a</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>o</mtext><mtext>d</mtext><mtext>d</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>f</mtext><mtext>u</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mtext>c</mtext><mtext>t</mtext><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>o</mtext><mtext>n</mtext> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow> </mrow> </math>

<math><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mo>[</mo><mo>−</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>0</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></span>

</details>

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> satisfies <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of integral <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Applying <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> property and adding, we get,
 <math><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the integral <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>I</mi><mi>n</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msubsup><mfrac><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>, then the value of <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>400</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>200</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>361</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>I</mi> <mrow> <mn>20</mn></mrow> </msub> <mo>&#8722;</mo><msub> <mi>I</mi> <mrow> <mn>19</mn></mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><msubsup> <mstyle> <mo>&#8747;</mo></mstyle> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>&#960;</mi> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </msubsup> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>sin</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>39</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>sin</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>37</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>20</mn></msub><mo>&#8722;</mo><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>19</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msubsup><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>38</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658;</mo><msub> <mi>I</mi> <mrow> <mn>20</mn></mrow> </msub> <mo>&#8722;</mo><msub> <mi>I</mi> <mrow> <mn>19</mn></mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><msubsup> <mstyle><mo>&#8747;</mo></mstyle> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>&#960;</mi> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </msubsup> <mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>38</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mfrac><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>38</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mn>19</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>38</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow></msub></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced close="]" open="["><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>20</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced close="]" open="["><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>19</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>I</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> (using <math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></math> property)
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Also, <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>

</details>

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the definite integral <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></munderover><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>&#949;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mi>n</mi></munderover><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>&#949;</mi></msub><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#949;</mi><mo>.</mo></math> (where,  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mo>&#8901;</mo></mfenced></math> is the greatest integer function), then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

The given integral is
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mn>1</mn></munderover><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></munderover><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></munderover><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></munderover><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
and <math><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></math> or <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q59. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>16</mn><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

Put <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></munderover><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2020</mn></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2020</mn></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2020</mn></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
Applying <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> property and adding, we get,
<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>16</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q60. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the integral <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>I</mi><mi>n</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mtext>π</mtext><mn>4</mn></mfrac></munderover><mfrac><mrow><mtext>sin</mtext><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mtext>sin</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow></msub></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>I</mi> <mrow> <mn>20</mn></mrow> </msub> <mo>&#8722;</mo><msub> <mi>I</mi> <mrow> <mn>19</mn></mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><munderover> <mstyle><mo>&#8747;</mo></mstyle> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>&#960;</mi> <mn>4</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </munderover> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>sin</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>39</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>sin</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>37</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>sin</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658;</mo><msub> <mi>I</mi> <mrow> <mn>20</mn></mrow> </msub> <mo>&#8722;</mo><msub> <mi>I</mi> <mrow> <mn>19</mn></mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><munderover> <mstyle><mo>&#8747;</mo></mstyle> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>&#960;</mi> <mn>4</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </munderover> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mtext>sin</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>x</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>cos</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>38</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>sin</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mfrac><mrow><mtext>sin</mtext><mfenced><mrow><mn>38</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mn>19</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>sin</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>38</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mn>19</mn></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q61. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="true">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>8</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The period of the function <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo></math> thus <math><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="true">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>x</mi><mi></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><munderover><mo stretchy="true">&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></munderover><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>As</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="true">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msubsup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></msubsup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

</details>

### Q62. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></munderover><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></munderover><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the greatest integer less than or equal to <math><mi>x</mi></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></munderover><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced open="|" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>e</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></munderover><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced open="|" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>e</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mi>e</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>
